# Image prompts (Nano Banana)

Auto-generated by `scripts/build_site.py`. Catalog entries load a real, openly licensed photo from Wikimedia Commons in the visitor's browser.
**Only generate an image when the site cannot find one.** Open the site's *Photo status* page (wiki/photo-status.html): it checks every entry live and lists exactly which ones still need a picture, with the prompt and a copy button.
Then save the image at the path shown and rerun the build. A local image always takes priority over the live photo.
Generate the image, save it at the path shown (PNG/JPG/WEBP all work, 16:9 recommended), then rerun the build; the placeholder is replaced automatically.

Total pending: **3191**

## Articles

### Natural Regions of the World and How They Connect
- Save as: `assets/img/articles/natural-regions-of-the-world.png`
```text
Editorial scientific illustration for an encyclopedia article titled "Natural Regions of the World and How They Connect": How climate, rock, soil, water and life combine into distinct natural regions, from the Indo-Gangetic Plain to the Sahara, and how to explore their links across this encyclopedia. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

### How Land Forms: Plates, Mountains and Erosion
- Save as: `assets/img/articles/how-land-forms.png`
```text
Editorial scientific illustration for an encyclopedia article titled "How Land Forms: Plates, Mountains and Erosion": Continents drift, collide and tear apart; rain, ice and wind wear them down. The two forces together build every landscape on Earth. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

### Mountains, Mountain Tops and Plateaus
- Save as: `assets/img/articles/mountains-and-plateaus.png`
```text
Editorial scientific illustration for an encyclopedia article titled "Mountains, Mountain Tops and Plateaus": How mountains rise, why their tops are cold and snowy, how altitude creates climate zones stacked like floors of a building, and why plateaus feed and water billions. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

### Deserts of the World: Hot, Cold, Coastal and Polar
- Save as: `assets/img/articles/deserts-of-the-world.png`
```text
Editorial scientific illustration for an encyclopedia article titled "Deserts of the World: Hot, Cold, Coastal and Polar": A desert is defined by dryness, not heat. Why deserts form, the five main kinds, how life and people survive in them, and how deserts are growing and shrinking. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

### Snow, Ice, Glaciers and Permafrost
- Save as: `assets/img/articles/snow-ice-and-permafrost.png`
```text
Editorial scientific illustration for an encyclopedia article titled "Snow, Ice, Glaciers and Permafrost": About a tenth of Earth's land lies under ice and a further quarter of the northern land is frozen underground. How the cryosphere works, why it is shrinking and what that means for water and sea level. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

### Forests, Grasslands and Wetlands: The Living Land
- Save as: `assets/img/articles/forests-grasslands-and-wetlands.png`
```text
Editorial scientific illustration for an encyclopedia article titled "Forests, Grasslands and Wetlands: The Living Land": The biomes that cover the green part of the land, from rainforest and taiga to savanna, steppe, mangroves and peat, how they work, and how much of each is left. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

### India's Land: Six Physical Divisions
- Save as: `assets/img/articles/india-physical-geography.png`
```text
Editorial scientific illustration for an encyclopedia article titled "India's Land: Six Physical Divisions": From the Himalaya to the Thar, the Indo-Gangetic Plain, the Deccan, the coasts and the islands, a tour of India's land, soils, rainfall and the crops each region grows. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

- Save as: `assets/img/articles/climate-zones-explained.png`

Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

- Save as: `assets/img/articles/rainfall-and-the-monsoon.png`

Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

- Save as: `assets/img/articles/climate-change-on-land.png`

```

How It Forms and Why It Matters": Soil is a living mix of minerals, water, air and organisms that takes centuries to form. Its layers, texture, the five factors that create it, and the 12 world soil orders. Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

```

- Save as: `assets/img/articles/land-degradation-and-desertification.png`

Photorealistic documentary photograph, natural light, 16:9, no text or labels
```

```
